  (local $2 i32)
  (local $3 i32)
  (local $4 i32)
  (local $5 i32)
  (local $6 i32)
  (local $7 i32)

   ;; code offset: 0x38d

     ;; code offset: 0x388

     ;; code offset: 0x38a

  ;; code offset: 0x391
  (block $block1
   ;; code offset: 0x393
   (block $block
    ;; code offset: 0x39a

     ;; code offset: 0x399

      ;; code offset: 0x395
      (local.get $0)
      ;; code offset: 0x397
      (i32.const 2)
     )
     (then
      ;; code offset: 0x3a8
      (br_if $block
       ;; code offset: 0x3a7
       (i32.gt_s
        ;; code offset: 0x3a3
        (local.tee $3
         ;; code offset: 0x3a1
         (call $atoi
          ;; code offset: 0x39e
          (i32.load offset=4
           ;; code offset: 0x39c
           (local.get $1)
          )
         )
        )
        ;; code offset: 0x3a5
        (i32.const 0)
       )
      )
     )
    )
    ;; code offset: 0x3b0
    (drop
     ;; code offset: 0x3ae
     (call $puts
      ;; code offset: 0x3ab

     )
    )
    ;; code offset: 0x3b3
    (local.set $5
     ;; code offset: 0x3b1
     (i32.const 1)
    )
    ;; code offset: 0x3b5
    (br $block1)
   )
   ;; code offset: 0x3bd
   (if
    ;; code offset: 0x3bc
    (i32.ne
     ;; code offset: 0x3b8
     (local.get $3)
     ;; code offset: 0x3ba
     (i32.const 1)
    )
    (then
     ;; code offset: 0x3c4

      ;; code offset: 0x3c3

       ;; code offset: 0x3bf
